🧭 World Models / WAMWorld Models / WAM

· 发展脉络· Development

世界模型（World Models）与动作世界模型（WAM）旨在通过预测未来状态或生成视频来构建环境的内部表征，从而赋能具身智能体的规划与决策。该领域从早期的像素级视频生成（如Genie）迅速演进至基于潜在空间的高效预测架构（如JEPA），并进一步聚焦于物理一致性、触觉感知及长程规划能力。近期发展呈现出向动作世界模型（WAM）转型的趋势，强调动作条件预测、3D几何理解及多模态融合，以解决传统生成模型缺乏物理因果性和规划可解释性的问题。World Models and World Models with Actions (WAM) aim to construct internal representations of environments by predicting future states or generating videos, thereby empowering embodied agents' planning and decision-making. This field has rapidly evolved from early pixel-level video generation (e.g., Genie) to efficient prediction architectures based on latent spaces (e.g., JEPA), and further focused on physical consistency, tactile perception, and long-horizon planning capabilities. Recent developments show a trend towards transformation into Action World Models (WAM), emphasizing action-conditioned prediction, 3D geometric understanding, and multimodal fusion to address the lack of physical causality and planning interpretability in traditional generative models.

切入角度Perspectives

  • 生成式视频驱动与自回归架构Generative Video-Driven and Autoregressive Architectures
  • 物理一致性与几何约束Physical consistency and geometric constraints
  • 世界动作模型 (WAM) 与零样本策略World Action Models (WAM) and Zero-Shot Policies
  • 具身多模态与 VLA 融合Embodied Multimodal and VLA Fusion
  • 高效推理与实时交互优化Efficient inference and real-time interaction optimization
  • 评测基准与幻觉分析Evaluation Benchmarks and Hallucination Analysis
  • 因果推断与多智能体建模Causal Inference and Multi-Agent Modeling
  • 结构化隐空间与代码世界模型Structured Latent Space and Code World Models
  • 理论极限与物理编码Theoretical Limits and Physical Encoding
  • 安全与鲁棒性Safety and Robustness

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

生成式视频驱动与自回归架构Generative Video-Driven and Autoregressive Architectures

该方向聚焦于利用自回归（AR）与扩散模型直接生成未来视频帧，构建像素级或潜空间的世界模型。核心动机在于通过生成式预测替代传统物理引擎，实现低成本、高保真的环境模拟。演进路径从早期MaskGIT等AR架构的初步尝试，转向混合架构（如Epona）以平衡画质与长时序一致性，并引入滚动窗口（Rolling Forcing）解决漂移问题。近期研究进一步探索实时交互（MoWorld, Matrix-Game 2.0）、显式记忆机制（MemoryWAM）及多模态控制，试图在生成质量、推理速度与物理逻辑之间寻找平衡，虽仍面临幻觉与物理一致性挑战，但已展现出作为通用具身智能训练环境的巨大潜力。This direction focuses on using autoregressive (AR) and diffusion models to directly generate future video frames, constructing pixel-level or latent-space world models. The core motivation lies in replacing traditional physics engines with generative prediction to achieve low-cost, high-fidelity environmental simulation. The evolutionary path has shifted from early attempts with AR architectures like MaskGIT to hybrid architectures (e.g., Epona) to balance image quality and long-term temporal consistency, while introducing Rolling Forcing to address drift. Recent research further explores real-time interaction (MoWorld, Matrix-Game 2.0), explicit memory mechanisms (MemoryWAM), and multimodal control, attempting to find a balance between generation quality, inference speed, and physical logic. Although still facing challenges of hallucination and physical consistency, it has demonstrated immense potential as a general-purpose training environment for embodied intelligence.

2026-09
SolarWM: Open Data and Scalable Training for Long-Horizon Video World Models
动机: Motivation: 解决世界模型领域数据标准不统一、异构数据难以融合以及长视界生成中累积误差难以消除的痛点。Addressing the pain points of non-uniform data standards in the world model field, the difficulty of fusing heterogeneous data, and the inability to eliminate cumulative errors in long-horizon generation.
方法: Method: 构建SolarWM开源体系，统一清洗10个数据集至25TB规范数据，并采用分布匹配（DMD）策略进行长视界因果训练。Constructing the SolarWM open-source system to unify and clean 10 datasets into 25TB of standardized data, employing a Distribution Matching (DMD) strategy for long-horizon causal training.
推进: Advance: 将世界模型训练从“炼丹”转向标准化工程，验证了统一数据标准对提升模型长时序一致性的关键作用，同时揭示了数据清洗成本高于模型训练的瓶颈。Shifting world model training from 'alchemy' to standardized engineering, validating the critical role of unified data standards in improving model long-horizon consistency, while revealing the bottleneck where data cleaning costs exceed model training costs.
2024-02
Genie: Generative Interactive Environments
动机: Motivation: 旨在验证纯视频数据能否让模型自学操作策略，摆脱对显式动作标签的依赖。Aims to verify whether pure video data can enable models to self-learn manipulation policies, eliminating reliance on explicit action labels.
方法: Method: 基于MaskGIT与VQ-VAE架构，利用11B参数模型通过自回归方式预测未来视频帧。Based on MaskGIT and VQ-VAE architectures, utilizing an 11B parameter model to predict future video frames via autoregression.
推进: Advance: 首次实现仅凭视频数据即可生成可交互环境，证明了视频生成模型具备隐式学习物理规则的能力。First to achieve the generation of interactive environments solely from video data, proving that video generation models possess the ability to implicitly learn physical rules.
2024-03
DriveDreamer-2: LLM-Enhanced World Models for Diverse Driving Video Generation
动机: Motivation: 解决自动驾驶中长尾场景（如鬼探头）数据稀缺问题，利用LLM增强生成多样性。Addresses data scarcity in long-tail scenarios for autonomous driving (e.g., jaywalking pedestrians), utilizing LLMs to enhance generation diversity.
方法: Method: 结合LLM生成轨迹与扩散模型，将文本指令转化为多视角高保真驾驶视频。Combines LLM-generated trajectories with diffusion models to convert text instructions into multi-view, high-fidelity driving videos.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了生成视频在复杂长尾场景下的分布覆盖度，为下游感知训练提供了高质量合成数据。Significantly improves the distribution coverage of generated videos in complex long-tail scenarios, providing high-quality synthetic data for downstream perception training.
2024-05
Vista: A Generalizable Driving World Model with High Fidelity and Versatile Controllability
动机: Motivation: 追求高保真度与多模态控制能力，解决现有驾驶世界模型分辨率低、控制单一的问题。Pursues high fidelity and multimodal control capabilities, addressing the issues of low resolution and single control modality in existing driving world models.
方法: Method: 基于SVD魔改，构建支持高分辨率（576x1024）与10Hz帧率的多模态控制世界模型。Based on modified SVD, constructs a multimodal control world model supporting high resolution (576x1024) and 10Hz frame rate.
推进: Advance: 实现了高保真视频预测与灵活控制，并创新性地利用世界模型本身作为强化学习的奖励函数。Achieves high-fidelity video prediction and flexible control, and innovatively utilizes the world model itself as the reward function for reinforcement learning.
2024-05
Diffusion for World Modeling: Visual Details Matter in Atari
动机: Motivation: 解决离散潜变量丢失细节的问题，利用扩散模型提升Atari游戏中的视觉预测精度。Addresses the loss of details due to discrete latent variables, utilizing diffusion models to improve visual prediction accuracy in Atari games.
方法: Method: 将扩散模型直接作为世界模型核心，在Atari 100k数据集上进行自回归预测训练。Directly employs diffusion models as the core of the world model, conducting autoregressive prediction training on the Atari 100k dataset.
推进: Advance: 在26个游戏中刷新了世界模型代理的得分纪录，证明了扩散模型在视觉细节保留上的优势。Breaks world model agent score records in 26 games, demonstrating the advantage of diffusion models in preserving visual details.
2024-05
Generative Camera Dolly: Extreme Monocular Dynamic Novel View Synthesis
动机: Motivation: 利用生成式先验解决单目动态新视角合成中的遮挡与几何重建难题。Utilizes generative priors to solve occlusion and geometric reconstruction challenges in monocular dynamic novel view synthesis.
方法: Method: 基于SVD先验，将单目动态新视角合成转化为视频翻译任务，实现极端视角变换。Based on SVD priors, transforms monocular dynamic novel view synthesis into a video translation task, achieving extreme viewpoint transformations.
推进: Advance: 实现了零样本下的极端视角泛化与遮挡脑补，展示了生成式模型在视觉特效领域的强大潜力。Achieves zero-shot extreme viewpoint generalization and occlusion inpainting, showcasing the powerful potential of generative models in the visual effects domain.
2024-12
Navigation World Models
动机: Motivation: 降低扩散Transformer的计算复杂度，实现高效且可扩展的具身导航世界模型。Reduces the computational complexity of diffusion Transformers, enabling an efficient and scalable embodied navigation world model.
方法: Method: 提出CDiT架构，将计算复杂度从二次降为线性，利用扩散Transformer预测未来画面。Proposes the CDiT architecture, reducing computational complexity from quadratic to linear, utilizing diffusion Transformers to predict future frames.
推进: Advance: 在保持10亿参数规模下实现4倍加速，显著提升了未知环境下的规划与想象能力。Achieves 4x acceleration while maintaining a 1-billion parameter scale, significantly enhancing planning and imagination capabilities in unknown environments.
2025-05
MAGI-1: Autoregressive Video Generation at Scale
动机: Motivation: 利用自回归架构重塑视频生成，解决长上下文建模与流式生成需求。Reshapes video generation using autoregressive architectures to address long-context modeling and streaming generation requirements.
方法: Method: 构建24B参数的自回归视频生成模型，采用Transformer VAE加速解码与分块策略。Constructs a 24B parameter autoregressive video generation model, employing Transformer VAE for accelerated decoding and chunking strategies.
推进: Advance: 支持400万token上下文与流式生成，在长时序视频生成的规模效应上取得了突破性进展。Supports 4 million token context and streaming generation, achieving breakthrough progress in the scale effects of long-term video generation.
2025-06
Epona: Autoregressive Diffusion World Model for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 兼顾扩散模型的高画质与自回归模型的长时序灵活性，解决长视频生成的幻觉问题。Balances the high image quality of diffusion models with the long-term temporal flexibility of autoregressive models, addressing hallucination issues in long video generation.
方法: Method: 提出时空解耦架构，用Transformer处理时间、Diffusion处理空间，并引入Chain-of-Forward训练。Proposes a spatio-temporal decoupled architecture, using Transformers for time and Diffusion for space, and introducing Chain-of-Forward training.
推进: Advance: 将视频生成长度从15秒扩展至2分钟，同时保持FVD指标领先，隐式学到了交通逻辑。Extends video generation length from 15 seconds to 2 minutes while maintaining leading FVD metrics, implicitly learning traffic logic.
2025-08
Matrix-Game 2.0: An Open-Source, Real-Time, and Streaming Interactive World Model
动机: Motivation: 填补实时交互世界模型的空白，解决自回归模型在长程生成中的误差累积问题。Fills the gap in real-time interactive world models, addressing error accumulation in autoregressive models during long-horizon generation.
方法: Method: 采用Self-Forcing机制与低分辨率视频生成，实现25 FPS的实时交互体验。Adopts Self-Forcing mechanisms and low-resolution video generation to achieve real-time interactive experiences at 25 FPS.
推进: Advance: 在低算力场景下实现了可玩的实时交互，验证了视频生成模型作为轻量级仿真器的可行性。Achieves playable real-time interaction under low-compute scenarios, validating the feasibility of video generation models as lightweight simulators.
2025-08
Genie Envisioner: A Unified World Foundation Platform for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 统一机器人操作的学习、评估与仿真，探索视频生成模型作为策略基座的潜力。Unifies learning, evaluation, and simulation for robotic manipulation, exploring the potential of video generation models as policy foundations.
方法: Method: 构建多视角视频扩散模型，结合流匹配解码动作，将世界模型直接用于控制策略。Constructs a multi-view video diffusion model, combining flow matching for action decoding to directly apply the world model to control policies.
推进: Advance: 提出了EWMBench评估标准，证明了视频生成先验在机器人操作任务中的指导价值。Proposes the EWMBench evaluation standard, demonstrating the guiding value of video generation priors in robotic manipulation tasks.
2025-09
Rolling Forcing: Autoregressive Long Video Diffusion in Real Time
动机: Motivation: 解决自回归长视频生成中的漂移问题，实现实时且稳定的长时序预测。Addresses drift issues in autoregressive long video generation, achieving real-time and stable long-term prediction.
方法: Method: 提出Rolling Forcing机制，利用滚动窗口与注意力Sink进行帧间纠错。Proposes the Rolling Forcing mechanism, utilizing rolling windows and attention sinks for inter-frame error correction.
推进: Advance: 实现了单卡16fps下的长视频生成且漂移几乎为零，显著提升了长程生成的稳定性。Achieves long video generation at 16fps on a single card with near-zero drift, significantly enhancing the stability of long-horizon generation.
2025-10
Emu3.5: Native Multimodal Models are World Learners
动机: Motivation: 探索超长视频生成与多模态世界学习，验证自回归预测在具身操作中的边界。Explores ultra-long video generation and multimodal world learning, validating the boundaries of autoregressive prediction in embodied manipulation.
方法: Method: 基于10万亿token训练量，利用DiDA技术并行化图片生成，构建多模态世界学习者。Based on 10 trillion token training volume, utilizes DiDA technology to parallelize image generation, constructing a multimodal world learner.
推进: Advance: 展示了超长上下文下的图文交错任务能力，但揭示了生成步骤描述与真实物理控制的差距。Demonstrates capabilities in image-text interleaved tasks under ultra-long contexts, but reveals the gap between generation step descriptions and real physical control.
2025-11
World Simulation with Video Foundation Models for Physical AI
动机: Motivation: 通过工业化数据清洗与Flow Matching统一多模态生成，提升物理AI的仿真精度。Enhances simulation accuracy for physical AI by unifying multimodal generation through industrial data cleaning and Flow Matching.
方法: Method: 利用Flow Matching架构，对海量视频数据进行严格筛选，结合RL微调对齐人类偏好。Leveraging the Flow Matching architecture, massive video data undergoes rigorous filtering, combined with RL fine-tuning to align with human preferences.
推进: Advance: 实现了小模型规模下的高性能物理交互，证明了高质量数据清洗对世界模型泛化的关键作用。Achieved high-performance physical interaction at small model scales, demonstrating the critical role of high-quality data cleaning in world model generalization.
2026-01
Cosmos Policy: Fine-Tuning Video Models for Visuomotor Control and Planning
动机: Motivation: 探索将视频生成模型直接转化为机器人策略的可行性，解决传统VLA模型的延迟问题。Explored the feasibility of directly converting video generation models into robot policies, addressing the latency issues of traditional VLA models.
方法: Method: 将动作、状态与价值函数编码进视频扩散模型的潜在帧，实现端到端控制。Encoded actions, states, and value functions into the latent frames of video diffusion models to achieve end-to-end control.
推进: Advance: 在LIBERO等仿真与真实任务中超越VLA模型，验证了视频先验在视觉运动控制中的强大能力。Surpassed VLA models in simulation and real-world tasks such as LIBERO, validating the powerful capabilities of video priors in visual-motor control.

Einstein World Models
动机: Motivation: 赋予LLM物理思维实验能力，将视频生成作为可检查的中间假设工具。Endowed LLMs with physical thought experiment capabilities, utilizing video generation as a checkable intermediate hypothesis tool.
方法: Method: 将视频生成器作为CoT的可视化补丁，通过视频rollout验证物理假设。Employed video generators as visual patches for CoT, verifying physical hypotheses through video rollout.
推进: Advance: 提供了可解释的中间推理过程，但受限于数据缺口与物理一致性，目前仍偏向辅助工具。Provided interpretable intermediate reasoning processes, yet currently remains an auxiliary tool due to limitations in data gaps and physical consistency.
2026-06
Neural Voxel Dynamics: Learning Implicit 3D Physics via Volumetric Feature Advection
动机: Motivation: 利用隐式3D体素空间统一模拟流体与刚体，解决2D视频生成的物理一致性问题。Unified simulation of fluids and rigid bodies using implicit 3D voxel spaces, resolving physical consistency issues in 2D video generation.
方法: Method: 提出Neural Voxel Dynamics，通过V-JEPA特征与体素推进学习隐式物理规律。Proposed Neural Voxel Dynamics, learning implicit physical laws through V-JEPA features and voxel propagation.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需传统物理引擎的流体与刚体统一模拟，但深度估计误差仍是主要瓶颈。Achieved unified simulation of fluids and rigid bodies without traditional physics engines, though depth estimation error remains the primary bottleneck.
2026-06
MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory
动机: Motivation: 解决世界模型长程记忆中的“金鱼效应”与“硬盘效应”矛盾，提升推理效率。Resolved the contradiction between the 'goldfish effect' and 'hard drive effect' in world model long-term memory, enhancing reasoning efficiency.
方法: Method: 提出MemoryWAM，利用gist tokens压缩历史与anchor frames锚定开头，模拟人类记忆机制。Proposed MemoryWAM, utilizing gist tokens to compress history and anchor frames to anchor the beginning, simulating human memory mechanisms.
推进: Advance: 在长程任务上显著提升了效率与一致性，证明了混合记忆机制在WAM中的有效性。Significantly improved efficiency and consistency in long-horizon tasks, proving the effectiveness of hybrid memory mechanisms in WAM.
2026-06
Holo-World: Unified Camera, Object and Weather Control for Video World Model
动机: Motivation: 实现相机、物体与天气的统一控制，解决多模态环境编辑的几何一致性问题。Achieved unified control of cameras, objects, and weather, resolving geometric consistency issues in multi-modal environment editing.
方法: Method: 提出UniSA架构，将世界保持与天气转移拆分为独立参数子空间，依赖深度先验。Proposed the UniSA architecture, decoupling world maintenance and weather transfer into independent parameter subspaces, relying on depth priors.
推进: Advance: 实现了从单图出发的多模态环境编辑，避免了背景结构偏移，提升了可控性。Enabled multi-modal environment editing starting from a single image, avoiding background structural shifts and enhancing controllability.
2026-06
ImageWAM: Do World Action Models Really Need Video Generation, or Just Image Editing?
动机: Motivation: 降低视频生成的算力消耗与延迟，探索以图代视的轻量级世界模型方案。Reduced computational consumption and latency in video generation, exploring lightweight world model solutions based on image-to-video paradigms.
方法: Method: 利用预训练图像编辑模型的KV Cache作为世界模型中间态，跳过视频解码过程。Utilized the KV Cache of pre-trained image editing models as the intermediate state of world models, bypassing the video decoding process.
推进: Advance: 将FLOPs与延迟分别降低至1/6与1/4，证明了图像编辑模型在特定场景下的高效性。Reduced FLOPs and latency to 1/6 and 1/4 respectively, demonstrating the efficiency of image editing models in specific scenarios.
2026-06
World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决具身智能在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，同时规避存储真实历史数据带来的隐私与内存成本。Aims to address the catastrophic forgetting problem in embodied intelligence during continuous learning while avoiding the privacy and memory costs associated with storing real historical data.
方法: Method: 利用世界动作模型（WAM）的生成能力，通过“生成式重放”在潜空间“脑补”过往任务轨迹以替代真实数据回放。Leverages the generative capabilities of World Action Models (WAM) to 'hallucinate' past task trajectories in latent space via 'generative replay' to replace real data replay.
推进: Advance: 证明了无需真实数据即可通过生成幻觉缓解遗忘，为终身学习提供了低资源替代方案，尽管长程生成质量存在衰减。Demonstrates that forgetting can be alleviated without real data through generative hallucinations, providing a low-resource alternative for lifelong learning, despite the degradation of long-horizon generation quality.
2026-06
Fast LeWorldModel
动机: Motivation: 针对自回归世界模型（LeWM）推理速度慢、长序列规划易累积误差的痛点，寻求实时性与精度的平衡。Addresses the pain points of slow inference in autoregressive world models (LeWM) and error accumulation in long-sequence planning, seeking a balance between real-time performance and accuracy.
方法: Method: 引入动作前缀（action-prefix）机制，将自回归的串行推演转化为并行预测，直接推断动作序列的累积效应。Introduces an action-prefix mechanism to transform autoregressive serial deduction into parallel prediction, directly inferring the cumulative effects of action sequences.
推进: Advance: 显著降低了规划时间并提升了成功率，验证了并行预测在特定仿真环境下的有效性，但牺牲了部分细粒度时序依赖。Significantly reduces planning time and improves success rates, validating the effectiveness of parallel prediction in specific simulation environments, but at the cost of some fine-grained temporal dependencies.
2026-07
WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory
动机: Motivation: 解决生成模型中的物体恒存性问题，通过显式控制维持长视频的一致性。Addressed the object permanence issue in generation models, maintaining long-video consistency through explicit control.
方法: Method: 利用LLM规划3D轨迹作为外挂，强行约束生成模型中的物体存在与运动。Utilized LLM-planned 3D trajectories as an external module to forcibly constrain object existence and motion within generation models.
推进: Advance: 显著改善了物体消失与变形的幻觉问题，证明了显式轨迹引导优于隐式记忆。Significantly improved hallucinations of object disappearance and deformation, proving that explicit trajectory guidance outperforms implicit memory.
2026-07
Valdi: Value Diffusion World Models
动机: Motivation: 探索扩散模型在实时规划循环中的价值，解决确定性模型的多模态缺失问题。Explored the value of diffusion models in real-time planning loops, addressing the multi-modal deficiency of deterministic models.
方法: Method: 提出Valdi，用单步扩散替代TD-MPC的确定性MLP，构建价值引导的扩散世界模型。Proposed Valdi, replacing the deterministic MLP of TD-MPC with single-step diffusion to construct a value-guided diffusion world model.
推进: Advance: 证明了扩散模型在在线控制中的可行性，但也揭示了多步去噪能力在规划中的局限性。Demonstrated the feasibility of diffusion models in online control, while also revealing limitations in multi-step denoising capabilities for planning.
2026-07

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决视频预测与动作预测共享骨干网络导致的任务干扰问题，避免模型因试图同时优化像素生成与策略决策而陷入“贪多嚼不烂”的困境。Aiming to address the task interference caused by video prediction and action prediction sharing a backbone network, avoiding the dilemma of the model attempting to simultaneously optimize pixel generation and policy decision-making, which leads to 'trying to eat too much and choking'.

WorldDiT: A Unified Diffusion Architecture for World and Action Modeling
动机: Motivation: 试图摆脱对千亿级视觉语言模型（VLM）的依赖，探索在中等参数规模下通过统一扩散架构同时实现高质量世界建模与机器人控制的可能性。Attempts to break free from reliance on trillion-parameter Vision-Language Models (VLM), exploring the possibility of achieving high-quality world modeling and robot control simultaneously through a unified diffusion architecture at a moderate parameter scale.
方法: Method: 构建基于Flow Matching的统一扩散架构（WorldDiT），在训练阶段引入RGB预测辅助任务以增强世界模型表征，而在推理阶段直接丢弃该预测头以专注于动作生成。Constructs a unified diffusion architecture (WorldDiT) based on Flow Matching, introducing an RGB prediction auxiliary task during training to enhance world model representation, while directly discarding this prediction head during inference to focus on action generation.
推进: Advance: 证明了无需超大模型即可在仿真基准上达到帕累托最优，通过“训练时画饼、推理时吃饼”的策略平衡了生成质量与推理效率，为轻量化世界模型提供了新的架构范式。Proves that Pareto optimality can be achieved on simulation benchmarks without ultra-large models, balancing generation quality and inference efficiency through the strategy of 'drawing pies during training and eating pies during inference', providing a new architectural paradigm for lightweight world models.

QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization
动机: Motivation: 针对现有潜空间世界模型中因经验预测（EP）导致的潜空间分布尾部膨胀及规划失效问题，旨在通过正则化手段修复潜空间分布以增强长时序预测的稳定性。Aiming to address the tail expansion of latent space distributions and planning failure caused by Experience Prediction (EP) in existing latent space world models, this work seeks to repair latent space distributions via regularization to enhance the stability of long-horizon predictions.
方法: Method: 提出基于分位数-分位数（QQ）匹配的正则化损失函数，并采用跨批次（Cross-Batch）策略在有限显存下实现大规模样本统计，以强制潜变量符合正态分布。Proposing a regularization loss function based on Quantile-Quantile (QQ) matching and employing a Cross-Batch strategy to achieve large-scale sample statistics under limited GPU memory, thereby enforcing latent variables to conform to a normal distribution.
推进: Advance: 从数学上证明了经验预测的局限性，通过简单的损失函数替换而非架构修改，有效抑制了潜空间漂移并解决了梯度消失问题，显著提升了控制任务的规划鲁棒性。Mathematically proving the limitations of Experience Prediction, this approach effectively suppresses latent space drift and resolves gradient vanishing issues through simple loss function substitution rather than architectural modification, significantly improving planning robustness in control tasks.

动机: Motivation: 为解决具身智能中动作与视觉生成的解耦难题，试图构建一种统一的动作流（Action Flow）架构，将机器人关节控制直接映射为像素级视频运动。To address the decoupling challenge between action and visual generation in embodied intelligence, this work attempts to construct a unified Action Flow architecture that directly maps robot joint control to pixel-level video motion.
方法: Method: 提出Hydra-0模型，利用逆模式（Inverse mode）从人类演示中反推机器人动作，并依赖Isaac Lab仿真数据生成训练流以统一世界建模与控制。Proposing the Hydra-0 model, which utilizes inverse mode to infer robot actions from human demonstrations and relies on Isaac Lab simulation data to generate training flows for unified world modeling and control.
推进: Advance: 相比基线Cosmos 2.5显著降低了预测误差（降幅达九成），验证了通过动作流统一通用世界建模与控制的可行性，尽管其通用性仍受仿真依赖限制。Significantly reducing prediction errors by 90% compared to the baseline Cosmos 2.5, validating the feasibility of unifying general world modeling and control through action flows, although its generalization remains limited by simulation dependency.

ActionSplice: In-Flight Action Editing for Interactive World Models
动机: Motivation: 解决自回归世界模型在交互场景中因用户中途修改动作指令而导致的画面崩坏或计算资源浪费问题，特别是现有方法在“硬切”或“回滚”时的不一致性。Addresses the issue of visual degradation or computational resource waste in interactive scenarios caused by users modifying action instructions mid-stream in autoregressive world models, particularly the inconsistency existing methods exhibit during 'hard cuts' or 'rollbacks'.
方法: Method: 提出反事实状态传输（CST）机制，利用全量回滚数据训练修正器，在生成过程中强行将模型状态“拽”至符合新动作指令的轨迹，而非等待或重头生成。Proposes a Counterfactual State Transfer (CST) mechanism that utilizes full rollback data to train a corrector, forcibly 'pulling' the model state to a trajectory compliant with new action instructions during generation, rather than waiting or regenerating from scratch.
推进: Advance: 实现了高效的在途动作编辑（In-Flight Action Editing），在保持生成速度的同时显著提升了长时序打断下的状态一致性，为实时交互提供了新范式。Achieves efficient In-Flight Action Editing, significantly enhancing state consistency under long-term interruptions while maintaining generation speed, providing a new paradigm for real-time interaction.

DIDO: Distilling Interaction-Centric Dynamics into One-Step Denoising for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 为了解决扩散模型在多步去噪过程中产生的动态滞后现象及由此引发的闭环控制延迟，试图在保持生成质量的同时实现单步实时预测。Aiming to solve the dynamic lag phenomenon generated during multi-step denoising in diffusion models and the resulting closed-loop control latency, this work attempts to achieve single-step real-time prediction while maintaining generation quality.

推进: Advance: 成功将预测延迟压缩至 384ms，在 LIBERO 基准上达到 SOTA 性能，证明了单步生成模型在复杂交互任务中具备实时控制的潜力。Successfully compresses prediction latency to 384ms, achieving SOTA performance on the LIBERO benchmark, demonstrating the potential of single-step generative models for real-time control in complex interaction tasks.

Honeycomb: Constant-Size Scene Memory Representation for Video World Models
动机: Motivation: 自回归视频世界模型在长序列生成中常面临显式记忆无限膨胀导致的计算效率低下及场景回看一致性下降的难题。Autoregressive video world models often face the challenges of low computational efficiency due to infinite expansion of explicit memory and declining scene rewatch consistency in long-sequence generation.
方法: Method: 提出Honeycomb架构，利用六个固定平面进行低秩分解与3D点反投影，构建恒定大小的场景记忆表示，拒绝无限膨胀的显式存储。Proposes the Honeycomb architecture, utilizing six fixed planes for low-rank decomposition and 3D point back-projection to construct a constant-sized scene memory representation, rejecting infinite expansion of explicit storage.
推进: Advance: 在保持恒定计算资源的前提下，显著提升了长时序场景的回看一致性，证明了固定大小内存表示在平衡生成质量与推理效率方面的有效性，为长程具身规划提供了新范式。Significantly improves the rewatch consistency of long-horizon scenes while maintaining constant computational resources, proving the effectiveness of fixed-size memory representations in balancing generation quality and inference efficiency, providing a new paradigm for long-horizon embodied planning.

物理一致性与几何约束Physical consistency and geometric constraints

该方向旨在解决生成式世界模型缺乏物理常识与几何一致性的核心痛点，从单纯的视频像素预测转向显式物理与几何约束的融合。早期研究通过基准测试（如Physics-IQ, VideoPhy-2）揭露了现有模型在OOD场景下违背牛顿定律的“案例匹配”本质。演进路径上，研究从引入显式物理先验（如对称性、守恒律）和几何结构（如BEV、4D Occupancy、3D点云）入手，逐步发展为将物理引擎、因果推理与扩散模型深度耦合。最新工作尝试在训练阶段注入几何先验或构建物理可编辑数据集，以在保持生成效率的同时，实现长程动力学一致性与真实世界的鲁棒迁移，推动世界模型从“视觉幻觉”向“物理模拟器”的质变。This direction aims to address the core pain point of generative world models lacking physical common sense and geometric consistency, shifting from simple video pixel prediction to the integration of explicit physical and geometric constraints. Early research exposed the 'case-matching' nature of existing models violating Newton's laws in OOD scenarios through benchmark tests (e.g., Physics-IQ, VideoPhy-2). On the evolutionary path, research started by introducing explicit physical priors (e.g., symmetry, conservation laws) and geometric structures (e.g., BEV, 4D Occupancy, 3D point clouds), gradually evolving into the deep coupling of physics engines, causal reasoning, and diffusion models. Latest works attempt to inject geometric priors during the training phase or construct physically editable datasets to achieve long-horizon dynamic consistency and robust transfer to the real world while maintaining generation efficiency, driving the qualitative leap of world models from 'visual hallucinations' to 'physical simulators'.

方法: Method: 引入Koopman算子理论对潜空间动力学施加谱约束，将非线性演化映射为线性算子空间以增强稳定性。Introducing Koopman operator theory to impose spectral constraints on latent space dynamics, mapping nonlinear evolution into a linear operator space to enhance stability.
推进: Advance: 在平滑动力学任务上显著提升了长视距预测的稳定性，通过调节谱半径实现了误差衰减与信息保留的可控平衡。Significantly improving the stability of long-horizon prediction on smooth dynamics tasks, achieving a controllable balance between error decay and information retention by adjusting the spectral radius.

方法: Method: 构建基于双曲几何的潜空间（ProWorld），利用其层级特性显式约束进度方向与全局几何结构。Constructing a hyperbolic geometry-based latent space (ProWorld), utilizing its hierarchical properties to explicitly constrain progress direction and global geometric structure.
推进: Advance: 在长视界任务（如AntMaze）中显著提升了规划成功率，证明了显式几何先验对解决长程规划一致性问题的有效性。Significantly improving planning success rates in long-horizon tasks (e.g., AntMaze), demonstrating the effectiveness of explicit geometric priors in solving long-range planning consistency issues.

推进: Advance: 以极小参数量在LIBERO基准上实现SOTA，证明了在策略空间内融合几何约束进行世界建模的可行性与高效性。Achieving SOTA on the LIBERO benchmark with a minimal parameter count, proving the feasibility and efficiency of fusing geometric constraints for world modeling within the policy space.

动机: Motivation: 长视界视频世界模型在长序列生成中因缺乏物理验证机制而产生累积漂移，无法保证动力学的一致性。Long-horizon video world models suffer from cumulative drift in long-sequence generation due to the lack of physical verification mechanisms, failing to guarantee dynamic consistency.

推进: Advance: 通过物理定律的恒等式约束有效抑制了长序列生成的漂移问题，为长视界模型提供了无需显式物理引擎的自验证机制。Effectively suppressing drift in long-sequence generation through identity constraints of physical laws, providing long-horizon models with a self-verification mechanism without requiring explicit physical engines.

动机: Motivation: 为了解决纯数据驱动模型缺乏物理约束以及传统神经辐射场（NeRF）每场景优化效率低下的痛点，试图构建兼具物理仿真能力与通用生成效率的4D世界模型。Attempting to construct a 4D world model combining both physical simulation capabilities and universal generation efficiency, addressing the pain points of pure data-driven models lacking physical constraints and the low efficiency of traditional Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) per-scene optimization.
方法: Method: 引入神经本构算子（Neural Intrinsic Dynamics Operator）将已知物理定律显式嵌入生成过程，结合Few-shot适配机制实现对新场景的快速物理属性推断。Introducing Neural Intrinsic Dynamics Operators to explicitly embed known physical laws into the generation process, combined with a few-shot adaptation mechanism to achieve rapid physical property inference for new scenes.
推进: Advance: 成功将物理信息算子与视觉生成模型深度耦合，在保持4D生成能力的同时实现了基于已知本构关系的动力学约束，填补了从专用仿真到通用物理世界模型的空白。Successfully deeply coupling physical information operators with visual generation models, achieving dynamic constraints based on known constitutive relationships while maintaining 4D generation capabilities, filling the gap from specialized simulation to universal physical world models.

PhysWAM: Physically Consistent World Action Model for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 针对现有自动驾驶世界模型缺乏物理灵魂、几何关系易产生幻觉的问题，旨在通过显式约束解决生成内容违背物理定律的痛点。Aiming to address the lack of physical soul in existing autonomous driving world models and the tendency for geometric relationships to produce hallucinations, this work seeks to solve the pain point of generated content violating physical laws through explicit constraints.
方法: Method: 提出耦合点投影（CPP）机制，将生成的深度与运动强行投影至3D空间并与LiDAR真值对齐，同时采用多轨迹采样中位数策略进行规划。Proposes the Coupled Point Projection (CPP) mechanism, forcibly projecting generated depth and motion into 3D space and aligning them with LiDAR ground truth, while adopting a median strategy in multi-trajectory sampling for planning.
推进: Advance: 通过LiDAR真值监督显著提升了规划阶段的空间几何一致性与安全性，证明了显式几何约束对抑制视觉幻觉的有效性。Significantly improves spatial geometric consistency and safety during the planning phase through LiDAR ground truth supervision, proving the effectiveness of explicit geometric constraints in suppressing visual hallucinations.

MVG-WAM: Multiple View Geometry-Aware World-Action Modeling for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为解决多视角世界模型将不同摄像头画面视为独立拼贴画、缺乏统一物理尺度常识的缺陷，试图引入严格的几何约束。To address the defect of multi-view world models treating different camera views as independent collages lacking unified physical scale common sense, this work attempts to introduce strict geometric constraints.
方法: Method: 利用对极几何与深度监督将多视角输入强制纳入几何约束框架，赋予模型物理尺度感知能力，无需额外具身预训练。Utilizes epipolar geometry and depth supervision to forcibly incorporate multi-view inputs into a geometric constraint framework, endowing the model with physical scale perception capabilities without requiring additional embodied pre-training.
推进: Advance: 在保持高任务成功率的同时，确立了多视角间的几何关联，证明了显式几何先验能有效解决多视角Token简单拼接导致的物理割裂问题。While maintaining high task success rates, establishes geometric correlations between multi-views, proving that explicit geometric priors can effectively solve the physical fragmentation problem caused by simple concatenation of multi-view tokens.

世界动作模型 (WAM) 与零样本策略World Action Models (WAM) and Zero-Shot Policies

本方向聚焦于将世界模型与动作空间深度耦合，构建“世界动作模型（WAM）”，旨在通过预测视频隐变量直接驱动策略，实现零样本规划与控制。早期工作如Think2Drive和DriveVLA-W0利用世界模型提供自监督信号以解决数据稀缺和物理规律理解问题；随后Motus、DreamZero等提出统一架构，将动作作为条件直接生成未来视频，实现“即插即用”的零样本策略；近期研究如Fast-WAM和VT-WAM进一步去魅，探索无需显式未来想象的高效推理及多模态（触觉/视觉）融合，推动具身智能从“试错学习”向“基于物理先验的脑补规划”演进，最终目标是构建无需大量任务微调即可泛化的通用机器人基座。This direction focuses on deeply coupling world models with action spaces to construct 'World Action Models (WAM)', aiming to drive policies directly by predicting video latent variables to achieve zero-shot planning and control. Early works like Think2Drive and DriveVLA-W0 utilize world models to provide self-supervised signals to address data scarcity and physical law understanding; subsequently, Motus and DreamZero propose unified architectures that generate future videos directly with actions as conditions, achieving 'plug-and-play' zero-shot policies; recent studies like Fast-WAM and VT-WAM further demystify, exploring efficient inference without explicit future imagination and multi-modal (tactile/visual) fusion, driving embodied intelligence from 'trial-and-error learning' to 'physics-prior-based mental simulation planning', with the ultimate goal of building a general-purpose robot foundation that generalizes without extensive task fine-tuning.

Spatially Aware World Action Model via Geometric Latent Diffusion
动机: Motivation: 针对现有WAM缺乏深度感知导致具身操作精度受限的问题，试图在不引入复杂3D编码器的情况下赋予模型空间理解能力。Attempting to endow models with spatial understanding capabilities without introducing complex 3D encoders to address the limitation in embodied manipulation accuracy caused by the lack of depth perception in existing WAMs.
方法: Method: 提出空间感知WAM（SA-WAM），通过非线性归一化将深度图强行嵌入冻结的VAE中，利用几何潜在扩散模型增强空间表征。Proposing a Spatially-Aware WAM (SA-WAM) that forcibly embeds depth maps into a frozen VAE via non-linear normalization, utilizing geometric latent diffusion models to enhance spatial representations.
推进: Advance: 在无需微调VAE的前提下显著提升了RoboCasa等任务的操控精度，验证了通过简单特征变换即可弥补WAM在3D空间感知上的短板，推动了物理先验的融合。Significantly improving manipulation accuracy in tasks such as RoboCasa without fine-tuning the VAE, verifying that simple feature transformations can compensate for WAM's shortcomings in 3D spatial perception and promoting the fusion of physical priors.

World-Coherent Decoding: Self-Verifying Test-Time Planning for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对WAM在长视界任务中因盲目多采样导致生成动作质量参差不齐、甚至出现物理幻觉的问题，旨在提升零样本规划的可信度。Aiming to improve the credibility of zero-shot planning by addressing the issue of inconsistent generation action quality and even physical hallucinations caused by blind multi-sampling in WAMs for long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 提出世界一致性解码（WCD）方法，利用生成过程中的内部信号（如视频惊讶度）在推理前对候选动作进行自我验证与打分，并引入执行反馈训练轻量级预测器。Proposing the World Consistency Decoding (WCD) method, which uses internal signals during the generation process (such as video surprise) to self-verify and score candidate actions before inference, and introduces execution feedback to train a lightweight predictor.
推进: Advance: 证明了在冻结骨干模型下，通过智能的推理筛选策略而非暴力增加采样次数，即可显著提升长视界任务的成功率，为WAM的零样本规划提供了高效的“事前预判”机制。Proving that under a frozen backbone model, intelligent inference filtering strategies rather than brute-force increases in sampling counts can significantly improve success rates in long-horizon tasks, providing an efficient 'pre-emptive judgment' mechanism for WAM zero-shot planning.

Sensorimotor World Models: Perception for Action via Inverse Dynamics

VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决触觉信号稀疏且易被视觉主导的问题，提升接触丰富任务中的操作成功率。It addresses the sparsity of tactile signals and their tendency to be dominated by vision, improving manipulation success rates in contact-rich tasks.
方法: Method: VT-WAM利用Flow Matching和不对称注意力，强制动作查询关注触觉序列，平衡多模态输入。VT-WAM utilizes Flow Matching and asymmetric attention to force action queries to focus on tactile sequences, balancing multimodal inputs.
推进: Advance: 在接触任务中成功率显著优于Fast-WAM，证明了在特定场景下，架构级的多模态平衡比单纯堆数据更有效。It achieves significantly higher success rates in contact tasks compared to Fast-WAM, proving that architecture-level multimodal balancing is more effective than simply stacking data in specific scenarios.

WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling
动机: Motivation: 解决实机强化学习（RL）数据收集成本高、试错风险大且效率低的问题。It addresses the high cost of data collection, significant trial-and-error risks, and low efficiency associated with real-world reinforcement learning (RL).
方法: Method: WorldSample利用世界模型在真实数据附近生成反事实轨迹，并采用策略-paced学习动态调整合成数据比例。WorldSample utilizes world models to generate counterfactual trajectories near real data and employs strategy-paced learning to dynamically adjust the proportion of synthetic data.
推进: Advance: 在现有框架下极大提升了数据效率，证明了“在真实数据附近做梦”比纯幻觉生成更可靠，适合资源受限团队。It greatly improves data efficiency under existing frameworks, proving that "dreaming near real data" is more reliable than pure hallucination generation, making it suitable for resource-constrained teams.

方法: Method: Bridge-WA将大模型蒸馏为紧凑先验，部署时仅保留轻量级预测器，实现轻量化世界感知。Bridge-WA distills large models into compact priors, retaining only lightweight predictors during deployment to achieve lightweight world perception.
推进: Advance: 证明了蒸馏后的轻量模型在OOD场景下仍能保持性能，为资源受限环境下的世界模型部署提供了可行方案。It demonstrates that distilled lightweight models can maintain performance in OOD scenarios, providing a feasible solution for world model deployment in resource-constrained environments.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有世界动作模型（WAM）在推理阶段计算冗余高、难以满足实时控制需求的问题，旨在通过架构解耦实现高效部署。Aiming to address the high computational redundancy during the inference stage of existing World Action Models (WAM) and their difficulty in meeting real-time control requirements, this work seeks to achieve efficient deployment through architectural decoupling.
方法: Method: 引入MoT架构将视觉与动作专家分离，训练时利用未来视频预测，推理时仅激活动作专家并配合AutoResearch自动调参。Introducing the MoT architecture to separate visual and action experts, utilizing future video prediction during training, and activating only the action expert during inference combined with AutoResearch for automatic hyperparameter tuning.

动机: Motivation: 针对潜世界模型在长视野规划中随机探索效率低、难以处理复杂长程任务的痛点，旨在通过结构化引导提升规划成功率。Aiming to improve planning success rates through structured guidance to address the pain points of latent world models, which suffer from low efficiency in random exploration and difficulty in handling complex long-horizon tasks during long-horizon planning.
方法: Method: 提出SAGE框架，先预测可达的中间子目标（subgoal），再基于该子目标在冻结的世界模型中生成具体动作序列。Proposing the SAGE framework, which first predicts reachable intermediate subgoals and then generates specific action sequences based on these subgoals within a frozen world model.

方法: Method: 提出“梦境强制”（Dream Forcing）策略，利用模型生成的视频训练逆动力学，并采用多层级训练架构。Proposing a "Dream Forcing" strategy that utilizes model-generated videos to train inverse dynamics, adopting a multi-level training architecture.
推进: Advance: 有效缓解了暴露偏差（Exposure Bias），在移动操作任务中实现了比纯 Teacher Forcing 更真实的长程规划能力。Effectively mitigating exposure bias, achieving more realistic long-range planning capabilities in mobile manipulation tasks compared to pure Teacher Forcing.

Is Energy Guidance All You Need? Training-Free Norm Injection for Driving World Models
动机: Motivation: 探索无需重新训练即可通过外部引导控制世界模型生成轨迹的方法，解决端到端可控性难题。Exploring methods to control world model trajectory generation via external guidance without retraining, solving the end-to-end controllability challenge.
方法: Method: 利用能量引导（Energy Guidance）在采样阶段强行扭转轨迹，尝试分离轨迹控制与视觉生成。Utilizing energy guidance to forcibly steer trajectories during the sampling phase, attempting to decouple trajectory control from visual generation.
推进: Advance: 揭示了当前双流架构中轨迹流与视觉流耦合不足的缺陷，指出了仅改轨迹不改画面的“假控制”风险。Revealing the defect of insufficient coupling between trajectory and visual streams in current dual-stream architectures, pointing out the risk of "fake control" where only trajectories are changed without altering visuals.

CoWAM: Coordination Contracts for Selective Policy Intervention with WAMs
动机: Motivation: 防止 WAM 直接干预动作时因过度预测导致的策略不稳定，探索保守且可审计的干预机制。Preventing policy instability caused by over-prediction when WAM directly intervenes in actions, exploring conservative and auditable intervention mechanisms.
方法: Method: 引入“协调合同”（Coordination Contracts）机制，规定 WAM 仅在特定条件下介入策略，否则保持冻结。Introducing a "Coordination Contracts" mechanism that stipulates WAM intervenes in the policy only under specific conditions, otherwise remaining frozen.
推进: Advance: 将策略的“选择”与“修正”解耦，为 WAM 在真实机器人系统中的安全部署提供了可解释的刹车机制。Decoupling the "selection" and "correction" of the policy, providing an interpretable braking mechanism for the safe deployment of WAM in real-world robotic systems.

Faster-WAM: Do World Action Models Need Deep Action Modules?
动机: Motivation: 质疑并优化 WAM 中动作模块的冗余设计，解决现有架构推理延迟高、泛化性差的问题。Questioning and optimizing the redundant design of action modules in WAM, addressing the issues of high inference latency and poor generalization in existing architectures.
方法: Method: 采用“轻头重身”架构，将动作头简化为单层，通过 Transformer 连接直接读取深层视频骨干特征。Adopting a "light-head heavy-body" architecture, simplifying the action head to a single layer and directly reading deep video backbone features via Transformer connections.
推进: Advance: 证明了动作预测无需深层网络堆叠，大幅降低了推理延迟并提升了模型在未见任务上的泛化能力。Demonstrating that action prediction does not require deep network stacking, significantly reducing inference latency and improving the model's generalization capability on unseen tasks.

方法: Method: 系统评估了多种异步部署方案，重点验证了训练时条件化（Training-time Conditioning）在真机上的表现。Systematically evaluating various asynchronous deployment schemes, focusing on verifying the performance of training-time conditioning on real machines.
推进: Advance: 揭示了工程排雷比算法创新更关键，确立了时间对齐和训练时条件化在实时控制中的核心地位。Revealing that engineering de-mining is more critical than algorithmic innovation, establishing the core status of time alignment and training-time conditioning in real-time control.

方法: Method: 提出自指导（Self-Guided）机制，将世界模型直接嵌入策略空间，利用几何监督引导注意力聚焦。Proposes a Self-Guided mechanism that directly embeds world models into the policy space, utilizing geometric supervision to guide attention focus.
推进: Advance: 证明了小参数模型在策略空间内建模的可行性，以低成本实现了媲美大模型的任务成功率。Demonstrates the feasibility of modeling within the policy space using small-parameter models, achieving task success rates comparable to large models at low cost.

推进: Advance: 在无需重训底座的情况下实现了动作修正，验证了任务空间规划对提升移动操作协调性的有效性。Achieves action correction without retraining the base model, validating the effectiveness of task space planning in improving mobile manipulation coordination.

DreamWAM: Beyond RGB Future Prediction for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 现有WAM过度沉迷像素级视频预测，忽略了动作控制本质所需的几何与语义结构，导致在视觉分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。Existing WAMs are overly obsessed with pixel-level video prediction, ignoring the geometric and semantic structures essential for action control, leading to insufficient robustness under visual distribution shifts.
方法: Method: 在训练阶段引入光流、深度和语义等多模态监督信号以强化表征，推理阶段则回归纯RGB部署，实现“训练作弊、推理守规”。Introducing multimodal supervision signals such as optical flow, depth, and semantics during the training phase to strengthen representations, while returning to pure RGB deployment during the inference phase, achieving "training cheating, inference compliance."
推进: Advance: 证明了动作相关的状态表征比单纯生成“好看的未来视频”更关键，显著提升了模型在视觉分布偏移下的性能与鲁棒性。Demonstrating that action-related state representations are more critical than simply generating "good-looking future videos," significantly improving model performance and robustness under visual distribution shifts.

MobileWAM: Bridging World Action Models to Mobile Manipulation with Chain-of-Foresight
动机: Motivation: 移动操作并非导航与操作的简单叠加，而是强耦合的因果过程，且现有WAM推理速度难以满足移动端实时性需求。Mobile manipulation is not a simple superposition of navigation and manipulation, but a strongly coupled causal process, and existing WAM inference speeds are difficult to meet the real-time requirements of mobile devices.
方法: Method: 提出“前瞻链”（Chain-of-Foresight）架构，训练时利用视频生成进行多步前瞻，推理时直接丢弃生成过程仅保留当前帧编码以加速。Proposing a "Chain-of-Foresight" architecture that utilizes video generation for multi-step foresight during training, but directly discards the generation process during inference, retaining only current frame encoding to accelerate.
推进: Advance: 实现了比Motus快5-8倍的推理速度，验证了移动端WAM的可行性，但也揭示了纯RGB输入在真机长任务中定位误差大的物理世界鸿沟。Achieving inference speeds 5-8 times faster than Motus, validating the feasibility of mobile WAMs, but also revealing the physical world gap of large localization errors with pure RGB input in real-machine long tasks.

动机: Motivation: 视频生成式WAM算力开销过大，不符合机器人实时控制需求，且大模型时代的语义理解并非控制精度的核心瓶颈。Video generative WAMs have excessive computational overhead, failing to meet robot real-time control needs, and semantic understanding in the era of large models is not the core bottleneck for control accuracy.
方法: Method: 采用冻结的DINOv3骨干网络，通过视觉过渡Token（VTT）替代语言模型，将算力集中用于未来预测与动作生成的联合训练。Adopting a frozen DINOv3 backbone network, replacing language models with Visual Transition Tokens (VTT), and concentrating computational power on joint training for future prediction and action generation.
推进: Advance: 成功构建了仅0.5B参数的轻量级WAM，证明了机器人控制无需庞大语义理解，只需精准的预测与动作映射即可实现高效零样本泛化。Successfully constructing a lightweight WAM with only 0.5B parameters, proving that robot control does not require massive semantic understanding, but only precise prediction and action mapping to achieve efficient zero-shot generalization.

动机: Motivation: 解决世界动作模型（WAM）在预训练动力学先验与视觉语义鲁棒性之间的冲突，避免昂贵的全量重训。Address the conflict between pre-trained dynamic priors and visual semantic robustness in World Action Models (WAM), avoiding expensive full-scale retraining.
方法: Method: 在动作流中注入可学习查询 Token，强制对齐未来帧的 DINOv3 语义特征，以修正 VAE 的视觉偏差。Inject learnable query Tokens into the action flow to forcibly align DINOv3 semantic features of future frames, correcting VAE visual biases.

动机: Motivation: 解决现有WAM过度关注短期物理细节而忽视长程任务逻辑（“只顾眼前不顾远方”）的问题。Addresses the problem that existing WAMs focus too much on short-term physical details while ignoring long-range task logic ("focusing on the present but ignoring the future").

HarnessWAM: Bridging Prediction and Deliberation in World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 弥合WAM作为“预测狂魔”在长程任务中出现的“预测- deliberation 鸿沟”，解决其难以处理复杂规划的问题。Bridges the "prediction-deliberation gap" that occurs in WAMs as "prediction maniacs" in long-range tasks, solving their difficulty in handling complex planning.

动机: Motivation: 打破WAM策略固定执行步数的僵化模式，解决不同任务难度下“步数少不安全、步数多低效”的矛盾。Breaks the rigid mode of fixed execution steps in WAM policies, solving the contradiction of "few steps are unsafe, many steps are inefficient" under different task difficulties.
方法: Method: 提出TempoWAM，设计基于任务进度的自适应执行机制，动态调整推理步数而非依赖动作熵猜测。Proposes TempoWAM, designing an adaptive execution mechanism based on task progress to dynamically adjust inference steps rather than relying on action entropy guessing.
推进: Advance: 实现了效率与成功率的动态平衡，在简单任务中显著减少计算开销，但强依赖“时间即进度”假设，在镜像或循环任务中监控机制会失效。Achieves a dynamic balance between efficiency and success rate, significantly reducing computational overhead in simple tasks, but heavily relies on the "time equals progress" assumption, causing the monitoring mechanism to fail in mirror or loop tasks.

推进: Advance: 证明了在隐空间进行动作后果预测比显式视频生成更高效，为黑盒策略提供了无需重训的实时“副驾驶”安全机制。Proved that predicting action consequences in latent space is more efficient than explicit video generation, providing a real-time 'co-pilot' safety mechanism for black-box strategies without retraining.

BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 解决VLA的语义规则理解与WAM的物理动态预测在统一架构中相互干扰、导致预测信号被语义信号压制的问题。Address the problem where semantic rule understanding in VLA and physical dynamic prediction in WAM interfere with each other in a unified architecture, causing prediction signals to be suppressed by semantic signals.
方法: Method: 提出BrainWAM架构，通过模拟人脑左右脑分工，利用胼胝体（CAB）和小脑（CIF）模块在动作空间实现语义先验与预测动态的解耦与握手。Propose the BrainWAM architecture, simulating the division of labor between the left and right human brains, utilizing Corpus Callosum (CAB) and Cerebellum (CIF) modules to achieve decoupling and handshaking of semantic priors and predictive dynamics in the action space.
推进: Advance: 在保持高预测精度的同时有效融合了多模态语义信息，验证了动作空间解耦融合是提升具身智能泛化性的有效路径。Effectively integrated multimodal semantic information while maintaining high prediction accuracy, verifying that decoupled fusion in the action space is an effective path to improve the generalization of embodied intelligence.

Foresight Without Seeing: Latent Futures for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 旨在消除显式视频生成带来的高计算延迟，探索在不生成未来视频的前提下，利用隐式状态实现动作模型对未来环境的“预见”能力。Aiming to eliminate the high computational latency introduced by explicit video generation, this work explores leveraging implicit states to enable action models to 'anticipate' future environments without generating future videos.
方法: Method: 设计Future-KV缓存机制，将视频DiT的中间隐状态直接注入动作DiT，利用隐式槽位替代迭代去噪过程以保留动态上下文。We design a Future-KV caching mechanism that directly injects intermediate implicit states from video DiT into action DiT, utilizing implicit slots to replace iterative denoising processes to preserve dynamic context.
推进: Advance: 实现了比Fast-WAM快14.8%的推理速度且参数量减半，证明了隐式状态传递是平衡零样本规划与实时性的实用折中方案。We achieve a 14.8% faster inference speed than Fast-WAM with half the parameter count, demonstrating that implicit state transfer is a practical compromise balancing zero-shot planning and real-time performance.
2026-08
Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对WAM因迭代去噪生成未来视频而导致的推理延迟高和计算资源消耗大的瓶颈，探索无需逐帧生成的快速推理策略。Addressing the bottleneck of high inference latency and substantial computational resource consumption in WAM caused by iterative denoising for future video generation, we explore fast inference strategies that do not require frame-by-frame generation.
方法: Method: 提出RIFT方法，利用学习到的预期token一次性填充K/V缓存，直接跳过迭代去噪步骤以加速未来状态预测。We propose the RIFT method, which utilizes learned expected tokens to fill the K/V cache in a single step, directly skipping iterative denoising steps to accelerate future state prediction.

动机: Motivation: 挑战世界动作模型必须依赖大规模视频生成大模型的观点，探索仅通过预测性视觉潜空间实现高效零样本策略的可行性。Challenges the view that world action models must rely on large-scale video generation large models, exploring the feasibility of achieving efficient zero-shot strategies solely through predictive visual latent spaces.

推进: Advance: 在 0.9B 参数下于 LIBERO 及真实双臂任务中超越部分带预训练的 VLA，证明了预测性表征在具身智能中的统治力，且无需生成器即可实现“脑补规划”。Surpasses some pre-trained VLA models with 0.9B parameters on LIBERO and real dual-arm tasks, proving the dominance of predictive representations in embodied intelligence, and achieving 'brain-computed planning' without a generator.

具身多模态与 VLA 融合Embodied Multimodal and VLA Fusion

本方向致力于将具身多模态感知与 VLA 范式深度融合，构建统一的世界动作模型（WAM）。核心思路是将视觉、语言、触觉及动作统一 Token 化，利用自回归或扩散架构实现“预测即规划”。演进路径从早期简单的 3D 滤镜或离散 Token 预测，转向解决多模态融合中的“触觉污染”、动作误差累积及物理一致性难题。最新研究通过不对称注意力、双系统架构及光流潜动作等机制，试图在保持实时性的同时，赋予模型长程预测与零样本泛化能力，推动具身智能从被动执行向主动认知演进。This direction is dedicated to deeply integrating embodied multimodal perception with the VLA paradigm to construct a unified World Action Model (WAM). The core idea is to unify vision, language, touch, and actions into tokens, utilizing autoregressive or diffusion architectures to achieve "prediction as planning." The evolutionary path has shifted from early simple 3D filters or discrete token prediction to addressing challenges such as "tactile contamination," action error accumulation, and physical consistency in multimodal fusion. Latest research attempts to endow models with long-horizon prediction and zero-shot generalization capabilities while maintaining real-time performance through mechanisms like asymmetric attention, dual-system architectures, and optical flow latent actions, driving embodied intelligence from passive execution to active cognition.

2024-03
3D-VLA: A 3D Vision-Language-Action Generative World Model
动机: Motivation: 针对传统 2D VLA 模型缺乏深度感知与未来预测能力的问题，试图通过引入 3D 世界模型来增强机器人的空间理解。Aiming to address the lack of depth perception and future prediction capabilities in traditional 2D VLA models, this work attempts to enhance robots' spatial understanding by introducing 3D world models.
方法: Method: 将 3D 点云与语言模型融合，利用扩散模型生成目标图像和点云，实现从 3D 数据到动作的生成式规划。Fusing 3D point clouds with language models, it utilizes diffusion models to generate target images and point clouds, achieving generative planning from 3D data to actions.
推进: Advance: 首次将 3D 生成式世界模型引入 VLA 框架，显著提升了模型在空间任务中的表现，但依赖预训练扩散模型仍存在幻觉风险。It is the first to introduce 3D generative world models into the VLA framework, significantly improving model performance in spatial tasks, yet relying on pre-trained diffusion models still poses hallucination risks.
2025-06
WorldVLA: Towards Autoregressive Action World Model
动机: Motivation: 针对自回归生成动作序列时误差累积导致策略崩溃的问题，试图通过架构优化提升动作生成的稳定性。Addressing the issue of policy collapse caused by error accumulation during autoregressive action sequence generation, it attempts to improve action generation stability through architectural optimization.
方法: Method: 设计注意力掩码策略，屏蔽历史动作对当前预测的干扰，构建自回归动作世界模型 WorldVLA。It designs an attention masking strategy to shield historical actions from interfering with current predictions, constructing the autoregressive action world model WorldVLA.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO 基准上优于 OpenVLA，证明了掩码策略能有效缓解误差累积，但动作离散化仍导致信息损失。Outperforming OpenVLA on the LIBERO benchmark, it proves that the masking strategy effectively mitigates error accumulation, yet action discretization still leads to information loss.
2025-07
DreamVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model Dreamed with Comprehensive World Knowledge
动机: Motivation: 为了降低世界模型的计算开销并聚焦关键信息，试图将未来预测从像素级重建降级为关键特征提取。To reduce the computational overhead of world models and focus on key information, it attempts to downgrade future prediction from pixel-level reconstruction to key feature extraction.
方法: Method: 提出 DreamVLA，不生成完整未来帧，而是直接预测物体运动、深度等关键信息，并移除推理时的预测头。It proposes DreamVLA, which does not generate complete future frames but directly predicts key information such as object motion and depth, while removing the prediction head during inference.
推进: Advance: 在 CALVIN 任务上实现了高效推理，证明了“关键信息提取”优于“像素重建”，但泛化性仍受限于 RGB 数据。It achieves efficient inference on CALVIN tasks, proving that "key feature extraction" outperforms "pixel reconstruction," yet generalization remains limited by RGB data.
2025-12
Motus: A Unified Latent Action World Model
动机: Motivation: 试图统一 VLA、世界模型等多种范式，解决具身智能中感知与决策割裂的问题。It attempts to unify paradigms such as VLA and world models to solve the problem of the disconnect between perception and decision-making in embodied intelligence.
方法: Method: 采用 MoT 架构结合光流潜动作，利用 Joint Attention 融合 VLM 与 VGM 专家，实现仿真与真机性能的统一。Adopting a MoT architecture combined with optical flow latent actions, it utilizes Joint Attention to fuse VLM and VGM experts, achieving unified performance in simulation and real-world settings.
推进: Advance: 在仿真与真机任务上均取得显著提升，是目前最接近通用人形机器人基础模型的尝试，但光流计算带来高延迟。It achieves significant improvements on both simulation and real-world tasks, representing the current attempt closest to a general-purpose humanoid robot foundation model, yet optical flow computation introduces high latency.
2026-01
Cosmos Policy: Fine-Tuning Video Models for Visuomotor Control and Planning
动机: Motivation: 探索利用预训练视频生成模型的强大先验，直接将其转化为机器人控制策略，简化架构设计。It explores leveraging the powerful priors of pre-trained video generation models to directly convert them into robot control policies, simplifying architectural design.
方法: Method: 将动作、状态和价值函数编码进视频扩散模型的潜在帧，通过微调视频模型实现视觉运动控制。It encodes actions, states, and value functions into the latent frames of a video diffusion model, achieving visual motion control through fine-tuning the video model.
推进: Advance: 在多项仿真与真实任务中达到 SOTA，验证了视频模型做控制的有效性，但推理延迟限制了其在动态场景的应用。It achieves SOTA performance in multiple simulation and real-world tasks, validating the effectiveness of using video models for control, yet inference latency limits its application in dynamic scenarios.
2026-02
World Action Models are Zero-shot Policies
动机: Motivation: 试图利用视频生成模型解决 VLA 在物理动力学建模上的短板，实现零样本泛化。It attempts to utilize video generation models to address the shortcomings of VLA in physical dynamics modeling, achieving zero-shot generalization.
方法: Method: 构建“世界动作模型”，利用视频生成质量决定策略上限，结合自回归架构与真值反馈缓解误差。It constructs a "World Action Model," where strategy upper bounds are determined by video generation quality, combining autoregressive architecture with ground truth feedback to mitigate errors.
推进: Advance: 实现了泛化能力提升 2 倍，证明了视频生成模型在零样本策略中的潜力，但高度依赖工程优化与特定硬件。It achieves a 2-fold improvement in generalization capability, proving the potential of video generation models in zero-shot policies, yet it relies heavily on engineering optimization and specific hardware.
2026-06
Not All Actions Are Equal: Rethinking Conditioning for Dexterous World Model
动机: Motivation: 针对现有世界模型在高自由度灵巧手操作中表现不佳的问题，指出动作编码粒度不均导致的优化困难。Addressing the poor performance of existing world models in high-degree-of-freedom dexterous hand operations, it points out the optimization difficulties caused by uneven action encoding granularity.
方法: Method: 提出结构化 Token 与局部全局双管齐下的编码方式，区分指尖微动与手腕大动作，优化模型对细粒度动作的感知。It proposes a structured token and dual local-global encoding approach, distinguishing fingertip micro-movements from wrist macro-movements to optimize the model's perception of fine-grained actions.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了灵巧手操作的成功率，证明了细粒度动作编码的重要性，但物理一致性仍依赖数据堆叠。It significantly improves the success rate of dexterous hand operations, proving the importance of fine-grained action encoding, yet physical consistency still relies on data stacking.

Risk-Aware Selective Multimodal Driver Monitoring with Driver-State World Modeling
动机: Motivation: 为了解决大模型 VLM 在实时驾驶监控中推理慢的问题，试图构建轻量级且能预测风险的世界模型。To solve the slow inference of large VLM models in real-time driving monitoring, it attempts to build a lightweight world model capable of predicting risks.
方法: Method: 结合 RGB 与生理信号的轻量学生模型，利用门控机制与“看情况办事”策略预测未来风险。It combines a lightweight student model with RGB and physiological signals, utilizing gating mechanisms and a "situation-dependent" strategy to predict future risks.
推进: Advance: 在视觉模糊场景下有效降低误报，证明了多模态融合在安全关键场景的实用性，但在极端情绪下存在校准漂移。It effectively reduces false alarms in visually blurred scenarios, proving the practicality of multi-modal fusion in safety-critical contexts, yet calibration drift exists under extreme emotional states.

Tactile-WAM: Touch-Aware World Action Model with Tactile Asymmetric Attention
动机: Motivation: 针对触觉数据直接输入视觉模型导致“触觉污染”的问题，试图隔离触觉与视觉的预测路径。Addressing the problem of "tactile pollution" caused by directly inputting tactile data into visual models, it attempts to isolate the prediction paths for tactile and visual modalities.
方法: Method: 提出 Tactile-WAM，利用不对称注意力（TAAM）与 VIDEOCLEAN 掩码，物理隔离触觉与视觉预测，仅动作通道接收触觉。It proposes Tactile-WAM, utilizing asymmetric attention (TAAM) and VIDEOCLEAN masking to physically isolate tactile and visual predictions, allowing only the action channel to receive tactile input.
推进: Advance: 在接触类任务中成功率大幅提升，成功解决了多模态融合中的信号干扰问题，但触觉监督仍依赖运动代理。It achieves a significant increase in success rate for contact-based tasks, successfully solving signal interference in multi-modal fusion, yet tactile supervision still relies on motion proxies.
2026-06
3D-DLP: Self-Supervised 3D Object-Centric Scene Representation Learning
动机: Motivation: 为了弥补 2D 模型在 3D 对象操作中的短板，试图构建基于对象中心的 3D 场景表示学习。To compensate for the shortcomings of 2D models in 3D object manipulation, it attempts to construct object-centric 3D scene representation learning.
方法: Method: 将 2D 深度潜粒子（DLP）扩展至 3D 体素空间，利用 K-means 改进关键点提议，实现自监督 3D 场景重构。It extends 2D depth latent particles (DLP) to 3D voxel space, utilizing K-means to improve keypoint proposals, achieving self-supervised 3D scene reconstruction.
推进: Advance: 在 MimicGen 等基准上优于 2D 基线，为下游策略提供了更鲁棒的 3D 输入，但体素化导致内存消耗大且动态场景适应性差。It outperforms 2D baselines on benchmarks such as MimicGen, providing more robust 3D inputs for downstream policies, yet voxelization leads to high memory consumption and poor adaptability to dynamic scenes.

VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对触觉数据在视觉霸权下被边缘化的问题，试图让触觉直接参与世界模型的构建以增强接触操作能力。Addressing the marginalization of tactile data under visual hegemony, it attempts to enable tactile data to directly participate in world model construction to enhance contact manipulation capabilities.
方法: Method: 利用 Flow Matching 融合视觉、触觉与动作，通过不对称注意力与 AVTAG 损失纠正视觉偏差。It utilizes Flow Matching to fuse visual, tactile, and action modalities, correcting visual bias through asymmetric attention and AVTAG loss.
推进: Advance: 在接触类任务中成功率显著提升，证明了触觉参与世界模型构建的必要性，但训练 - 推理不一致与扩展性仍是挑战。It achieves a significant improvement in success rate for contact-based tasks, proving the necessity of tactile participation in world model construction, yet training-inference inconsistency and scalability remain challenges.

动机: Motivation: 挑战“数据越多越好”的直觉，探索在零售具身智能中第三人称视角对世界模型适配的必要性。Challenging the intuition that "more data is better," it explores the necessity of third-person perspectives for world model adaptation in retail embodied intelligence.
方法: Method: 构建 RetailSMV 数据集，仅使用第三人称视频适配基础视频世界模型，对比混合视角训练效果。Construct the RetailSMV dataset, adapting only third-person videos to a foundational video world model, and compare the effects of mixed-viewpoint training.
推进: Advance: 发现第三人称视角在同步数据下效果优于混合视角，为多视角世界模型的数据选择提供了新启发，但泛化性待验证。Discover that third-person viewpoints outperform mixed viewpoints under synchronized data, providing new insights for data selection in multi-viewpoint world models, though generalizability remains to be verified.

方法: Method: 利用 41 个相机与触觉手套采集 215 小时真实数据，对比 2D 视频模型与 3D 粒子模型在变形物体上的表现。Utilize 41 cameras and tactile gloves to collect 215 hours of real-world data, comparing the performance of 2D video models and 3D particle models on deformable objects.
推进: Advance: 提供了里程碑式的大规模多模态数据集，验证了 3D 粒子模型在数据少时的稳定性，但触觉数据质量与实时性仍受限。Provide a milestone large-scale multimodal dataset, verifying the stability of 3D particle models under data scarcity, though tactile data quality and real-time performance remain limited.

推进: Advance: 在仿真环境中实现了多智能体实时互动与长程预测，证明了自编码器架构在动态场景的优势，但缺乏人类博弈的多样性。Achieve real-time multi-agent interaction and long-horizon prediction in simulation environments, demonstrating the advantages of the autoencoder architecture in dynamic scenarios, though lacking the diversity of human gameplay.

ABot-3DWorld 0: A Universal World Model to Explore Any 3D Space
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决现有世界模型难以在任意3D空间中实现通用探索与长程物理一致性的问题，试图打破纯2D视频生成的局限。Aims to solve the problem that existing world models struggle to achieve universal exploration and long-horizon physical consistency in arbitrary 3D spaces, attempting to break the limitations of pure 2D video generation.
方法: Method: 提出ABot-3DWorld 0，通过全景图与点云结合的SGP折中方案，将文本、图像及视频统一Token化并映射为3D高斯溅射（3DGS）表示。Proposes ABot-3DWorld 0, using an SGP compromise solution combining panoramic views and point clouds to unify text, images, and videos into tokens and map them to 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) representations.
推进: Advance: 实现了从多模态输入到原生地理坐标锚定3D场景的端到端生成，证明了在保持地图实用性的同时，利用合成数据训练可显著提升3D空间探索的泛化能力。Achieves end-to-end generation from multimodal inputs to natively geospatially anchored 3D scenes, proving that training with synthetic data can significantly enhance the generalization capability of 3D spatial exploration while maintaining map practicality.

方法: Method: 构建380亿参数的多模态自回归模型，通过统一目标函数将具身生成与基础图像生成“缝合”，保留预训练视觉知识并适配机器人数据引擎。Constructs a 38-billion-parameter multimodal autoregressive model, "stitching" embodied generation and foundational image generation through a unified objective function, preserving pre-trained visual knowledge and adapting to robot data engines.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有具身世界模型缺乏统一中间表示导致规划与执行割裂，以及VLA架构推理延迟高的问题，旨在构建一个能融合感知、规划与仿真的全栈式中间表示套件。Aiming to construct a full-stack intermediate representation suite that integrates perception, planning, and simulation to address the fragmentation between planning and execution caused by the lack of a unified intermediate representation in existing embodied world models, as well as the high inference latency of VLA architectures.
方法: Method: 提出RoboInter1.5，利用230k多模态片段构建细粒度中间表示，结合控制视频扩散模型实现物理一致性预测，并采用模块化VLA架构处理多模态Token。Proposing RoboInter1.5, which utilizes 230k multi-modal segments to construct fine-grained intermediate representations, combines control video diffusion models for physically consistent prediction, and employs a modular VLA architecture to process multi-modal tokens.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了具身任务中物理动态的预测精度与多模态数据的细粒度对齐能力，为具身智能提供了高质量的基础设施，尽管在OOD泛化上仍需验证。Significantly improving the prediction accuracy of physical dynamics in embodied tasks and the fine-grained alignment capability of multi-modal data, providing high-quality infrastructure for embodied intelligence, although generalization on OOD data still requires verification.

World Translation: Minimizing Sim-to-Real Gap with Backward Dynamics Extraction and Unpaired Domain Translation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决传统世界模型在Sim-to-Real迁移中因正向动力学预测累积误差导致的仿真器依赖及物理不一致难题，特别是接触事件等不可预测场景。To address the challenges of simulator dependence and physical inconsistency in Sim-to-Real transfer caused by cumulative errors in forward dynamics prediction by traditional world models, particularly in unpredictable scenarios such as contact events.
方法: Method: 引入“事后诸葛亮”机制，通过逆向动力学提取（Backward Dynamics Extraction）从结果反推隐藏变量，利用非配对数据训练模拟真实世界与仿真世界的转换。Introducing a 'hindsight' mechanism that extracts hidden variables via Backward Dynamics Extraction from results, utilizing unpaired data to train the conversion between the real world and the simulation world.
推进: Advance: 在接触事件等历史观测不足的场景下，比RSSM等依赖历史状态的模型更具鲁棒性，有效缓解了部分Sim-to-Real鸿沟，但高度依赖仿真器的物理真实性。Demonstrating greater robustness than history-dependent models like RSSM in scenarios with insufficient historical observations, such as contact events, effectively mitigating part of the Sim-to-Real gap, though highly dependent on the physical fidelity of the simulator.

动机: Motivation: 针对手术场景下具身感知中视觉编码器冻结导致噪声保留或全量微调丢失空间细节的矛盾，以及现有VLM在细粒度动作识别上的局限性。Addressing the contradiction between noise retention due to frozen visual encoders or loss of spatial details due to full fine-tuning in embodied perception for surgical scenarios, as well as the limitations of existing VLMs in fine-grained action recognition.
方法: Method: 提出LAViFiT框架，利用逆动力学模型（IDM）和正向世界模型（FWM）引导视觉微调，并引入SIG正则化防止特征坍塌，专注于动作相关区域的动态感知。Proposing the LAViFiT framework, which utilizes Inverse Dynamics Models (IDM) and Forward World Models (FWM) to guide visual fine-tuning, introduces SIG regularization to prevent feature collapse, and focuses on dynamic perception of action-related regions.
推进: Advance: 成功将世界模型机制融入手术动作识别的感知层，在CholecT50等数据集上提升了细粒度识别的mAP，实现了从被动分类向动态感知引导的初步演进。Successfully integrating world model mechanisms into the perception layer of surgical action recognition, improving mAP for fine-grained recognition on datasets like CholecT50, and achieving a preliminary evolution from passive classification to dynamic perception guidance.

DeVA: Decoupled Video-Action Model with physical guidance for robot policy learning
动机: Motivation: 针对VLA与视频动作模型（VAM）共享骨干网络导致的“贪多嚼不烂”及物理一致性缺失问题，旨在解耦视频预测与动作规划以提升策略学习的物理可靠性。Aiming to address the 'trying to eat too much and choking' and lack of physical consistency caused by VLA and Video Action Models (VAM) sharing a backbone network, it seeks to decouple video prediction and action planning to enhance the physical reliability of policy learning.
方法: Method: 提出DeVA架构，将视频预测与动作预测解耦为双专家系统，并引入可 affordance 和深度信息的物理引导机制来约束动作生成。Proposes the DeVA architecture, decoupling video prediction and action prediction into a dual-expert system, and introducing a physical guidance mechanism based on affordance and depth information to constrain action generation.
推进: Advance: 通过物理引导显著提升了机器人在复杂操作中的成功率（RoboCasa达72%），证明了分离视频与动作预测路径能有效避免多模态融合中的性能干扰。Significantly improves the success rate of robots in complex manipulation through physical guidance (reaching 72% on RoboCasa), proving that separating video and action prediction paths effectively avoids performance interference in multi-modal fusion.

推进: Advance: 实现了首个大规模触觉原生世界动作模型，通过触觉事件切分机制有效提升了长程接触任务的规划能力，填补了纯视觉模型在精细手感操作上的空白。Realizes the first large-scale tactile-native world action model, effectively improving planning capabilities for long-horizon contact tasks through a tactile event segmentation mechanism, filling the gap of pure visual models in fine-grained haptic manipulation.

CheckVLA: Execution-Time Verification with Action-Conditioned World Model for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation

CogWAM: Aligning Semantic Cognition with World Action Modeling via Event-Driven Interfaces
动机: Motivation: 为了解决传统VLA模型在长程规划中因连续帧重算导致的算力浪费及状态判断“精神分裂”问题。To address the computational waste caused by continuous frame recalculation and the 'schizophrenia' problem of state judgment in long-horizon planning of traditional VLA models.
方法: Method: 引入事件驱动接口与语义状态机，仅在关键任务阶段或状态变更时触发世界动作模型的更新与推理。Introduces event-driven interfaces and semantic state machines, triggering updates and inference of the world action model only during key task stages or state changes.
推进: Advance: 在无需预训练的情况下实现了语义认知与动作建模的高效对齐，显著提升了模型在复杂物理交互中的工程落地效率。Achieves efficient alignment of semantic cognition and action modeling without pre-training, significantly improving the engineering deployment efficiency of the model in complex physical interactions.

高效推理与实时交互优化Efficient inference and real-time interaction optimization

本方向聚焦于解决视频生成类世界模型（WAM）在具身交互中推理延迟高、计算开销大的核心痛点。演进脉络从早期的架构剪枝与蒸馏（如Fast-WAM、ImageWAM）转向更精细的时序优化（如Rolling Forcing、MemoryWAM）及多阶段解耦生成（如DVG-WM）。核心思路在于打破“高保真视频生成”与“实时控制”的对立，通过牺牲部分视觉细节、利用几何先验、引入混合记忆机制或重构推理范式（如去未来想象），在边缘设备或低算力场景下实现毫秒级响应。这一过程标志着世界模型从“离线高保真模拟”向“在线实时交互工具”的务实转型。This direction focuses on addressing the core pain points of high inference latency and large computational overhead in video-generation-based world models (WAM) for embodied interaction. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from early architectural pruning and distillation (e.g., Fast-WAM, ImageWAM) to more fine-grained temporal optimization (e.g., Rolling Forcing, MemoryWAM) and multi-stage decoupled generation (e.g., DVG-WM). The core logic lies in breaking the opposition between 'high-fidelity video generation' and 'real-time control' by sacrificing some visual details, leveraging geometric priors, introducing hybrid memory mechanisms, or reconstructing the inference paradigm (e.g., removing future imagination), thereby achieving millisecond-level responses on edge devices or low-compute scenarios. This process marks a pragmatic transformation of world models from 'offline high-fidelity simulation' to 'online real-time interaction tools'.

推进: Advance: 在保留六摄像头空间感知能力的同时，彻底消除了实时推理中的视频生成延迟，实现了从“生成式想象”到“判别式决策”的高效范式转换。While retaining six-camera spatial perception capabilities, this work completely eliminates video generation latency in real-time inference, achieving an efficient paradigm shift from 'generative imagination' to 'discriminative decision-making'.

Beyond a single latent space: a dual-latent world model for long-horizon planning
动机: Motivation: 单一潜空间难以同时兼顾局部执行精度与长视界规划的稳定性，导致递归误差累积及目标区分度下降。A single latent space struggles to simultaneously balance local execution precision and long-horizon planning stability, leading to recursive error accumulation and reduced target discriminability.
方法: Method: 构建双潜空间架构解耦局部执行与长期规划，结合LoRe加权Rollout与MAPS正则化优化搜索过程。Construct a dual latent space architecture to decouple local execution from long-term planning, combining LoRe-weighted Rollout with MAPS regularization to optimize the search process.
推进: Advance: 有效缓解了长视界任务中的距离坍缩问题，在保持规划稳定性的同时提升了复杂任务下的泛化表现。Effectively alleviates the distance collapse problem in long-horizon tasks, enhancing generalization performance under complex tasks while maintaining planning stability.

评测基准与幻觉分析Evaluation Benchmarks and Hallucination Analysis

本角度聚焦于揭示当前世界模型（尤其是视频生成与具身智能）在物理常识、长程一致性及幻觉问题上的本质缺陷。核心思路是从盲目追求生成逼真度转向构建基于物理定律、状态持久性及因果逻辑的严格评测体系。演进脉络显示：早期工作（如WorldSimBench）试图建立基础视频评测，随后研究（Physics-IQ, VideoPhy-2）深入量化物理直觉缺失，指出模型仅是“案例匹配器”；进而（WRBench, AGI Maze）揭露模型缺乏持久状态表征与空间推理能力；最新研究（Hallucination Predictable, iKCE）将幻觉归因于数据分布与动力学建模缺失，并提出从运动学自洽向动力学真实跨越的诊断指标，强调数据质量与物理引擎的必要性。This perspective focuses on revealing the fundamental defects of current world models (especially in video generation and embodied intelligence) regarding physical commonsense, long-horizon consistency, and hallucination issues. The core idea is to shift from blindly pursuing generation realism to constructing a rigorous evaluation system based on physical laws, state persistence, and causal logic. The evolutionary trajectory shows: early works (e.g., WorldSimBench) attempted to establish basic video evaluation; subsequent research (Physics-IQ, VideoPhy-2) deeply quantified the lack of physical intuition, pointing out that models are merely 'case matchers'; further studies (WRBench, AGI Maze) exposed the lack of persistent state representation and spatial reasoning capabilities; latest research (Hallucination Predictable, iKCE) attributed hallucinations to data distribution and dynamics modeling deficiencies, proposing diagnostic metrics for the leap from kinematic self-consistency to dynamic authenticity, emphasizing the necessity of data quality and physics engines.

2026-09
Discriminative World Models for Web Agents
动机: Motivation: 批判当前Web Agent世界模型仅作为“案例匹配器”盲目模仿HTML格式，导致无法捕捉动作引发的关键状态差异及深层幻觉。Critiquing current Web Agent world models for merely acting as 'case matchers' that blindly imitate HTML formats, leading to an inability to capture key state differences triggered by actions and deep hallucinations.
方法: Method: 提出“预测状态匹配”判别式方法，摒弃对固定格式生成的依赖，转而利用LLM Judge评估动作前后的状态语义差异。Proposing a 'predict state matching' discriminative method that abandons reliance on fixed-format generation, instead utilizing LLM Judge to evaluate semantic differences in states before and after actions.
推进: Advance: 证明了从生成式转向判别式评估能有效识别模型对关键状态变化的感知盲区，但也暴露了依赖同类模型作为Judge可能引入的评估自洽性幻觉。Demonstrating that shifting from generative to discriminative evaluation can effectively identify model blind spots regarding key state changes, but also exposes the risk of evaluation self-consistency hallucinations introduced by relying on similar models as Judges.

The World Model Remembers, the Actor Forgets: Dream Rehearsal for Continual Model-Based RL
动机: Motivation: 针对具身智能中世界模型虽能记忆但执行者（Actor）遗忘的“通道问题”，指出单纯保护世界模型无法解决灾难性遗忘，需重新审视想象轨迹的利用方式。Address the 'channel problem' in embodied intelligence where world models can remember but the actor forgets, pointing out that simply protecting the world model cannot solve catastrophic forgetting, requiring a re-examination of how imagined trajectories are utilized.

推进: Advance: 得出“未来视频内容不重要，时间结构才重要”的反直觉结论，证明了简单架构在捕捉时间动力学上的有效性，为物理一致性建模指明方向。Derives the counter-intuitive conclusion that 'future video content is unimportant, temporal structure is important', proving the effectiveness of simple architectures in capturing temporal dynamics and pointing the way for physical consistency modeling.

方法: Method: 提出EVO-WAM框架，利用VLM和IDM作为内部裁判，通过自生成的视频动作对迭代优化模型，无需外部执行器即可在仿真中验证动作逻辑。Proposes the EVO-WAM framework, utilizing VLM and IDM as internal judges to iteratively optimize the model via self-generated video actions, verifying action logic in simulation without external actuators.
推进: Advance: 揭示了仅依赖仿真随机化可能导致实机性能过拟合的隐患，同时证明了利用大VLM进行内部验证虽能提升仿真表现，但验证器的准确性直接决定了模型进化的上限与物理真实性。Reveals the hidden danger that relying solely on simulation randomization may lead to overfitting on real machines, while proving that although using large VLMs for internal verification improves simulation performance, the accuracy of the verifier directly determines the upper limit of model evolution and physical authenticity.

因果推断与多智能体建模Causal Inference and Multi-Agent Modeling

在因果推断与多智能体建模视角下，世界模型（WAM）正从单纯拟合统计分布转向构建可干预的因果结构。核心思路是利用多智能体演示数据，通过显式建模干预机制（如软干预）来剥离混杂因素，从而区分相关性与因果性。演进路径上，早期工作多关注单智能体预测，近期研究（如ICWM）则聚焦于多智能体交互中的因果图挖掘，试图解决仿真与真实世界的泛化鸿沟。尽管理论证明干预能提升结构恢复，但实际应用中仍面临布局外推困难及干预策略在物理实体上难以实施的挑战，未来需平衡因果图精度与模型鲁棒性。From the perspective of causal inference and multi-agent modeling, world models (WAM) are shifting from merely fitting statistical distributions to constructing intervenable causal structures. The core idea is to utilize multi-agent demonstration data, explicitly modeling intervention mechanisms (e.g., soft interventions) to disentangle confounding factors, thereby distinguishing correlation from causality. In terms of evolution, early work focused on single-agent prediction, while recent research (e.g., ICWM) focuses on mining causal graphs in multi-agent interactions, attempting to bridge the generalization gap between simulation and the real world. Although theoretical proofs show interventions improve structure recovery, practical applications still face challenges in out-of-layout extrapolation and the difficulty of implementing intervention strategies on physical entities, requiring a future balance between causal graph accuracy and model robustness.

Do-JEPA: From Masking to Intervention in Latent World Models
动机: Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型多依赖遮挡观察进行预测，难以区分动作带来的统计相关性与真实的因果效应，导致模型缺乏对物理干预的深层理解。Existing latent world models mostly rely on occluded observation for prediction, making it difficult to distinguish statistical correlations caused by actions from true causal effects, resulting in a lack of deep understanding of physical interventions.
方法: Method: 提出Do-JEPA框架，将C-JEPA的遮挡机制升级为显式的物理干预，利用模拟器生成对比数据以分离混杂因素并精准定位动作入口。Proposes the Do-JEPA framework, upgrading the occlusion mechanism of C-JEPA to explicit physical intervention, utilizing simulators to generate contrastive data to separate confounding factors and precisely locate action entry points.
推进: Advance: 成功从观测数据中剥离出因果效应并恢复传播图，证明了从“遮挡预测”转向“干预学习”能显著提升模型对动作因果机制的识别能力。Successfully disentangles causal effects from observational data and recovers the propagation graph, proving that shifting from occlusion prediction to intervention learning significantly enhances the model's ability to identify causal mechanisms of actions.

结构化隐空间与代码世界模型Structured Latent Space and Code World Models

该方向旨在解决传统世界模型隐空间缺乏可解释性与物理一致性的问题，核心思路是将结构化代码（如场景图、物理参数）作为隐空间基底，替代纯神经黑盒。演进路径从依赖预提取的结构信息（如VPW），逐步向结合混合引擎与端到端学习发展，试图在保持规划可解释性的同时，提升对复杂物理交互的泛化能力。This direction aims to solve the lack of interpretability and physical consistency in traditional world model latent spaces. The core idea is to use structured code (e.g., scene graphs, physical parameters) as the latent space basis, replacing pure neural black boxes. The evolution path moves from relying on pre-extracted structural information (e.g., VPW) towards combining hybrid engines with end-to-end learning, attempting to maintain planning interpretability while improving generalization for complex physical interactions.
