方法: Method: 提出AnisoWM，引入可学习的各向异性目标函数替代传统的SIGReg正则化，显式修正预测空间与规划空间之间的几何扭曲，无需改动预测器与规划器架构。Proposes AnisoWM, introducing a learnable anisotropic objective function to replace traditional SIGReg regularization, explicitly correcting geometric distortions between prediction and planning spaces without modifying predictor and planner architectures.
推进: Advance: 在多个物理环境中显著提升了规划成功率，证明了在结构化隐空间中引入各向异性约束能有效解决特征空间与物理动作空间的不一致，为提升规划鲁棒性提供了新的理论视角。Significantly improves planning success rates in multiple physical environments, proving that introducing anisotropic constraints in structured latent spaces effectively resolves inconsistencies between feature spaces and physical action spaces, providing a new theoretical perspective for enhancing planning robustness.

理论极限与物理编码Theoretical Limits and Physical Encoding

本角度聚焦于具身智能中世界模型（World Models）在物理编码层面的理论极限。核心思路在于质疑经典计算架构在模拟复杂物理世界时的根本性瓶颈，特别是内存大小与表征保真度之间的非线性关系。演进路径从经典统计学习转向探索物理定律（如量子力学）对信息处理的硬约束。该方向旨在揭示：单纯堆砌经典算力无法突破特定物理场景下的预测误差下界，必须引入新的物理编码范式（如量子态）才能逼近真实世界的动态演化，从而为具身智能的模型构建确立不可逾越的物理边界与新的理论基石。This perspective focuses on the theoretical limits of World Models in embodied intelligence at the level of physical encoding. The core idea questions the fundamental bottlenecks of classical computing architectures in simulating complex physical worlds, particularly the non-linear relationship between memory size and representation fidelity. The evolutionary path shifts from classical statistical learning to exploring hard constraints on information processing imposed by physical laws (e.g., quantum mechanics). This direction aims to reveal that simply piling up classical computing power cannot break the lower bound of prediction error in specific physical scenarios; new physical encoding paradigms (e.g., quantum states) must be introduced to approximate the dynamic evolution of the real world, thereby establishing insurmountable physical boundaries and new theoretical foundations for the construction of embodied intelligence models.

安全与鲁棒性Safety and Robustness

在具身智能的安全与鲁棒性视角下，世界模型（World Models）的研究正从单纯的场景预测转向防御机制与风险感知的双重构建。早期工作多关注模型在理想环境下的预测精度，而近期演进聚焦于两大核心挑战：一是如何量化并预测动态交互中的碰撞风险，二是如何防御针对预训练模型的隐蔽攻击。CorrRisk-WM 通过走廊条件化机制，将安全预测从全局重建中解耦，实现了高效的风险雷达；而当世界模型被广泛复用为供应链组件时，Backdoor Attacks 揭示了潜在动力学被恶意篡改的致命漏洞。这一领域的演进逻辑是从“被动预测”转向“主动防御”与“风险感知”，强调在复杂交互和对抗环境下，模型必须具备对非反应性行为的预判能力以及对数据投毒的免疫机制，从而构建可信的具身决策闭环。From the perspective of safety and robustness in embodied intelligence, research on World Models is shifting from simple scene prediction to the dual construction of defense mechanisms and risk perception. Early work focused mainly on prediction accuracy under ideal environments, while recent evolution focuses on two core challenges: how to quantify and predict collision risks in dynamic interactions, and how to defend against covert attacks on pre-trained models. CorrRisk-WM decouples safety prediction from global reconstruction through a corridor-conditioned mechanism, achieving an efficient risk radar; when world models are widely reused as supply chain components, Backdoor Attacks reveal fatal vulnerabilities where potential dynamics are maliciously tampered with. The evolutionary logic of this field shifts from 'passive prediction' to 'active defense' and 'risk perception', emphasizing that in complex interactive and adversarial environments, models must possess the ability to predict non-reactive behaviors and immunity mechanisms against data poisoning, thereby constructing a trustworthy embodied decision loop.

Feeling Terrain Before Crossing: World Models for Off-Road Navigation
动机: Motivation: 针对传统视觉主导的导航世界模型在野外非结构化地形中因缺乏本体感知而导致的翻车风险，试图增强模型对物理交互的“体感”预测。Addressing the rollover risk caused by the lack of proprioception in traditional vision-dominated navigation world models in wild unstructured terrains, attempting to enhance the model's 'proprioceptive' prediction of physical interactions.
方法: Method: 设计 Feel-WM，将 IMU 数据与编码器信息融合，显式预测机器人在跨越地形时的本体感觉与物理反馈。Designing Feel-WM to fuse IMU data with encoder information, explicitly predicting the robot's proprioception and physical feedback when crossing terrains.
推进: Advance: 成功将本体感知引入世界模型，显著降低了野外导航的翻车率，验证了多模态融合在提升非结构化环境鲁棒性方面的关键作用。Successfully introducing proprioception into world models, significantly reducing rollover rates in wild navigation and validating the key role of multi-modal fusion in improving robustness in unstructured environments.
2026-09
Continual Enterprise World Model Discovery in Dynamic Systems
动机: Motivation: 应对企业级 Agent 在动态系统中面临的规则黑盒化及持续学习中对“否定性反馈”处理失效的鲁棒性挑战。Addressing the robustness challenges faced by enterprise-level agents in dynamic systems, including rule black-boxing and the failure to handle 'negative feedback' during continuous learning.
方法: Method: 提出 CDA 框架，构建可解释的持续进化世界模型，尝试将隐性规则转化为显式代码表示以优化决策逻辑。Proposing the CDA framework to construct an interpretable continuously evolving world model, attempting to convert implicit rules into explicit code representations to optimize decision logic.
推进: Advance: 提升了复杂规则迷宫中的预测准确率与可解释性，但暴露了模型在处理规则消失与隐性依赖时的逻辑硬伤，揭示了当前持续学习在动态环境下的鲁棒性瓶颈。Improving prediction accuracy and interpretability in complex rule mazes, but exposing logical flaws in the model when handling rule disappearance and implicit dependencies, revealing the robustness bottleneck of current continuous learning in dynamic environments.
